00191: # cabinet/vertical proportions: crown width is close, but counter and middle
00192: # shelves appear too high. Hypothesis: lower body is too tall relative to hutch.
00193: # Geometry family: shorten base about floor, extend hutch about crown.
00194: # Predict lower counter and shelf lines without camera or finish changes.
00195: # Dependencies: transform contents with hutch; preserve base-floor contact.
00196: base_factor=DIM['lower_height_factor']
00197: crown=DIM['crown']
00198: counter_center=1.055
00199: upper_factor=(crown-base_factor*counter_center)/(crown-counter_center)
00200: for key,node in cabinet.members.items():
00201:     if key in lower_keys:
00202:         node.resize((1,1,base_factor),pivot=(0,0,0))
00203:     else:
00204:         node.resize((1,1,upper_factor),pivot=(0,0,crown))
00205: china.resize((1,1,upper_factor),pivot=(0,0,crown))
00206: winder.resize((1,1,upper_factor),pivot=(0,0,crown))
00207: # Set the loose winder's feet onto the revised counter surface.
00208: counter_surface=(1.055+.075/2)*base_factor
00209: winder_bottom=(1.12-.035/2-crown)*upper_factor+crown
00210: winder.move((0,0,counter_surface-winder_bottom))

00212: scene.include('china_display',china)
00213: scene.include('tabletop_yarn_winder',winder)
